 )\n        \"\"\"\n    )\n    return {(row[\"invoice_id\"], row[\"contract_id\"]) for row in rows}\n\n\ndef derive_invoice_contract_candidates(\n    tv: TaskView,\n    clusters: list[set[str]],\n) ->
